Remove punctuation and extra spaces\n    name = re.sub(r'[.,\\-]', ' ', name)\n    name = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', name)\n    return name.strip()\n\n\ndef fuzzy_match(name1, name2, threshold=0.85):\n    \"\"\"Check if two vendor names are similar enough to be considered a match.\"\"\"\n    norm1 = normalize_vendor_name(name1)\n    norm2 = normalize_vendor_name(name2)\n    return SequenceMatcher(None, norm1, norm2).ratio() >= threshold\n\n\ndef extract_invoice_data_from_pdf(pdf_path):\n    \"\"\"Extract invoice data from PDF (one invoice per page).\"\"\"\n    invoices = []\n\n    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:\n        for page_num, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, start=1):\n            text = page.extract_text()\n\n            # Extract PO number (look for \"PO:\" label, may be optional)\n            po_match = re.search(r'PO:\\s*([A-Z0-9\\-]+)', text, re.IGNORECASE)\n            po_number = po_match.group(1).strip() if po_match else None\n\n            invoices.append({\n                'page_number': page_num,\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'amount': amount,\n                'iban': iban,\n                'po_number': po_number\n            })\n\n    return invoices\n\n\ndef extract_vendor_data_from_excel(excel_path):\n    \"\"\"Extract vendor data from Excel file.\"\"\"\n    vendors = {}\n\n    wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(excel_path)\n    sheet = wb.active\n\n    # First try exact match (case-insensitive)\n    for vendor_id, vendor_data in vendors.items():\n        if vendor_data['name'].lower() == invoice_vendor_name.lower():\n            return vendor_id, vendor_data\n\n    # Try fuzzy matching\n    for vendor_id, vendor_data in vendors.items():\n        if fuzzy_match(invoice_vendor_name, vendor_data['name']):\n            return vendor_id, vendor_data\n\n    return None, None\n\n\ndef identify_fraud(invoices, vendors, purchase_orders):\n    \"\"\"Identify fraudulent invoices based on criteria.\"\"\"\n    fraudulent = []\n\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        vendor_name = invoice['vendor_name']\n        invoice_amount = invoice['amount']\n        invoice_iban = invoice['iban']\n        po_number = invoice['po_number']\n\n        # Skip if essential data is missing\n        if not vendor_name:\n            continue\n\n        # Check fraud criteria in order\n\n        # 1. Unknown Vendor: vendor name not in vendors.xlsx\n        vendor_id, vendor_data = find_matching_vendor(vendor_name, vendors)\n\n        if vendor_id is None:\n            fraudulent.append({\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Unknown Vendor'\n            })\n            continue\n\n        # 2. IBAN Mismatch: vendor exists but IBAN doesn't match\n        if invoice_iban and vendor_data['iban'] and invoice_iban != vendor_data['iban']:\n            fraudulent.append({\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'IBAN Mismatch'\n            })\n            continue\n\n        # If no PO number on invoice, skip further checks\n        if not po_number:\n            continue\n\n        # 3. Invalid PO: PO number doesn't exist in purchase_orders.csv\n        if po_number not in purchase_orders:\n            fraudulent.append({\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Invalid PO'\n            })\n            continue\n\n        po_data = purchase_orders[po_number]\n\n        # 4. Amount Mismatch: PO exists but amount differs by more than 0.01\n        if invoice_amount is not None and po_data['amount'] is not None:\n            if abs(invoice_amount - po_data['amount']) > 0.01:\n                fraudulent.append({\n                    'invoice_page_number': invoice['page_number'],\n                    'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                    'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                    'iban': invoice_iban,\n                    'po_number': po_number,\n                    'reason': 'Amount Mismatch'\n                })\n                continue\n\n        # 5. Vendor Mismatch: PO valid but linked to different Vendor ID\n        if po_data['vendor_id'] != vendor_id:\n            fraudulent.append({\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Vendor Mismatch'\n            })\n            continue\n\n    return fraudulent\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to run fraud analysis.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Extracting invoice data from PDF...\")\n    invoices = extract_invoice_data_from_pdf('/root/invoices.pdf')\n    print(f\"Extracted {len(invoices)} invoices\")\n\n    print(\"Extracting vendor data from Excel...\")\n    vendors = extract_vendor_data_from_excel('/root/vendors.xlsx')\n    print(f\"Extracted {len(vendors)} vendors\")\n\n    print(\"Extracting purchase order data from CSV...\")\n    purchase_orders = extract_po_data_from_csv('/root/purchase_orders.csv')\n    print(f\"Extracted {len(purchase_orders)} purchase orders\")\n\n    print(\"Identifying fraudulent invoices...\")\n    fraudulent_invoices = identify_fraud(invoices, vendors, purchase_orders)\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraudulent_invoices)} fraudulent invoices\")\n\n    #
